  Cypher (1st attempt): MATCH (bp:BodyPart)-[:BODYPARTOVEREXPRESSESGENE]->(g:Gene)

  Cypher (final):       MATCH (g:Gene)-[:BODYPARTOVEREXPRESSESGENE]->(bp:BodyPart)

Q [accb2231e66f]: Which of the following is not a gene commonly associated with the cellular components cell surface and cell division site ? 1. RAB11A 2. PPP1CC 3. RALA 4. RBMY1A1 5. PSTPIP1
  Expected: 4
  Got:      0
  Thinking: I queried the KG for the five candidate genes linked to the CellularComponent nodes 'cell surface' and 'cell division site'. The aggregate query returned PPP1CC, PSTPIP1, RAB11A and RALA as having associations to both components (result_id 91e6ceb6-3a19-449c-834f-df8b19921f55). RBMY1A1 was not present in that list, so it is the only option not associated with both components in the KG (see result_id 0644db1a-8b00-47ce-b0b4-308969fd9b2a for the detailed associations).
  Cypher (1st attempt): MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:GENEASSOCIATEDWITHCELLULARCOMPONENT]->(c:CellularComponent)
WHERE g.geneSymbol IN ['RAB11A','PPP1CC','RALA','RBMY1A1','PSTPIP1']
AND c.commonName IN ['cell surface','cell division site']
RETURN g.geneSymbol AS gene, c.commonName AS component, count(r) AS rels
ORDER BY gene, component
  Cypher (final):       MATCH (g:Gene)-[:GENEASSOCIATEDWITHCELLULARCOMPONENT]->(c:CellularComponent)
WHERE c.commonName IN ['cell surface','cell division site']
AND g.geneSymbol IN ['RAB11A','PPP1CC','RALA','RBMY1A1','PSTPIP1']
WITH g.geneSymbol AS gene, collect(DISTINCT c.commonName) AS comps
WHERE ALL(x IN ['cell surface','cell division site'] WHERE x IN comps)
RETURN gene
ORDER BY gene
  Correction iters:     1

Q [64a8540e018a]: Which of the following genes is not commonly associated with the diseases Alzheimer's Disease and Alzheimer Disease, Early Onset ? 1. CRH 2. HLA-DRB5 3. MIR100 4. LOC127406786 5. GAPDHS
  Expected: 4
  Got:      0
  Thinking: I queried the KG for which of the five candidate genes are associated with both “Alzheimer's Disease” and “Alzheimer Disease, Early Onset”. The Cypher result (result_id 0fb10bac-dbb7-4f31-83a5-1e9ab3d7e236) returns MIR100, HLA-DRB5, CRH, and GAPDHS as associated with both diseases. The only option from the list not returned by that query (i.e., not present in the KG as associated with those diseases) is LOC127406786, so that is the correct answer under the KG’s closed-world assumption.
  Cypher (1st attempt): MATCH (d1:Disease {commonName:"Alzheimer's Disease"}), (d2:Disease {commonName:"Alzheimer Disease, Early Onset"})
MATCH (g:Gene)-[:GENEASSOCIATESWITHDISEASE]->(d1)
MATCH (g)-[:GENEASSOCIATESWITHDISEASE]->(d2)
RETURN g.geneSymbol AS geneSymbol, g.nodeID AS nodeID
LIMIT 200
  Cypher (final):       MATCH (g:Gene)-[:GENEASSOCIATESWITHDISEASE]->(d:Disease) WHERE g.geneSymbol IN ["CRH","HLA-DRB5","MIR100","LOC127406786","GAPDHS"] AND d.commonName IN ["Alzheimer's Disease","Alzheimer Disease, Early Onset"]
RETURN g.geneSymbol AS geneSymbol, collect(DISTINCT d.commonName) AS diseases, count(DISTINCT d) AS numDiseases
  Correction iters:     1

  Got:      5

OR4R3P and PAG1 have no over-expression hits in either tissue in this query.
